# Repository file index

This index inventories repository files retained as source or project artifacts. Paths are relative to the repository root. Generated dependency/build directories (node_modules, dist, and test-results) are excluded. Update this file whenever files are added, removed, renamed, or moved. Reference materials are grouped under reference/.

| Path | Description |
| --- | --- |
| [`AGENTS.md`](<AGENTS.md>) | Repository operating rules for agents and contributors. Milestone 72F: section 1 records the FAT32 drive's effect on browser-test timing, the current quick-tier count, and that a copy on C: can't run git against the repository. Milestone 72 final regression: section 1's note on the mixed-version test from a C: copy corrected -- it fails there only when no run on D: has unpacked the old build yet. 2026-10-06 (73F part 3): section 4: an impossible date joins the issues that can't be overridden; the blank-schedule rule now lives in the shared checks' prompts and covers the Inventory (Pinellas Clerk practice). 2026-10-08 (73S): section 6: the Plan for Minors is identified by its Case # first (73S-N2, the requester's named approval). |

| [`help\index.html`](<help/index.html>) | HTML entry point or help content. Updated 2026-10-03 to the app as of Milestone 72: filing without an attorney, who prepared the filing, the Ward's % rules and negative amounts, the certificate of service (the ward's status, how the copies were served, the filing attorney's details, old details), what an Excel import keeps, Review recommended warnings, Excel capacity, what carries over, case-file safety and the Activity Log; 67 figures re-shot from the capture harness and one added. Figures re-shot again the same day after df16d94 (the password dialog's wording) and 3645819 (the sidebar total), so they show both. 2026-10-06 (73I): the Annual Plan's due date adds April 1 for calendar-year filing; the plan covers the coming year. 2026-10-07 (73T part 2): the Initial Inventory's import: one confirmation, Cancel changes nothing, and a notice of what was kept and what came back as the workbook holds it. 2026-10-07 (73T part 3): Part XI carries into Excel one entry per line; the Annual family's import confirms first and keeps the filing's type. 2026-10-07 (73T part 4): every financial filing's import confirms first; the Simplified's Guardian #1 and its recipients while none are required. 2026-10-07 (73M with 74C): the export buttons are always clickable and say why; what the Excel workbook won't carry, and Save as Excel's question; how an import answers the bond question. 2026-10-07 (74P): the C-1 helper, the joint-owner button and the "same as" boxes. 2026-10-08 (73O part 2, 74F): the plain question and a name and four address lines on every certificate. 2026-10-08 (73G part 2, 74H): the sign and share notes, and the contradiction warnings under "Review recommended". 2026-10-08 (73H): one way to show amounts and dates. 2026-10-08 (73S): the UCN as a starred reminder; the Plan for Minors' Case # required. 2026-10-09 (73O part 4, 74J): two quotes of changed wording. 2026-10-09 (73P, 74S): "Save case file as…"; "pages to fill in". |

| [`MILESTONE-71-PROPOSAL.md`](<MILESTONE-71-PROPOSAL.md>) | Milestone 71 proposal (decisions settled 2026-09-29, including after two rounds of independent review; build approved 2026-09-29 -- progress in each item's Build record; records the risks accepted): 71A measures in Excel how the Clerk's workbook displays half cents; 71B makes attorney fields optional on the Inventory and Annual/Final/Trust/Simplified Accountings, asks the basis, and lets the guardian who served the copies sign the certificate of service (flag on the guardian row; Rules 2.515 and 2.516 quoted); 71C adds a real 0-100 percent field across every form write path and reads imported share cells as the workbook stores them; 71D gives Ward's % one blank-share rule (0%, printed as blank) and fixes the D-1/Help instructions; 71E adopts one workbook-matching rounding rule and one carried-ending-balance function for all five carry paths, never carries the estate into or out of a Trust Accounting, and keeps negative and $0 Starting Balances. Its one OPEN item, 71B's printed no-attorney sentence, closed 2026-10-01: none is printed (Milestone 72D). 72G added a correction note to 71B step 5 (the method box). |
| [`MILESTONE-72-PROPOSAL.md`](<MILESTONE-72-PROPOSAL.md>) | Milestone 72 proposal (approved for build 2026-10-02 and built item by item, each with its Build record -- 72A to 72J built (all ten items), with a final full regression 2026-10-03; decisions by the requester, a Pinellas Clerk representative, 2026-10-01/02, recorded as Clerk practice where they concern what the office accepts; revised four times on 2026-10-02 after three rounds of review by Codex and one by Antigravity, all recorded in the proposal): 72A puts the Inventory's Excel Part III guardian details in their boxes and guards every export (no caption overwritten and no formula changed; every box the exporters write gets a typed test value checked in its expected cell, from a manifest with a completeness check on unlisted writes, and extra exports so Yes/No and dropdown boxes can't swap unnoticed); 72B requires an Inventory share on every started row, keeps a 0 share as 0 on every path, requires the Inventory attorney's email, and keeps both attorney emails on every form through models, data model, carry-over and conversion; 72C gives every accounting and Plan the same guardian-email rule (warn, never block, only without an attorney), requires the attorney's name on the Annual family and Annual Plan and the Plan for Minors' attorney email once an attorney is entered, keeps the Simplified Plan's attorney optional, and fixes the Simplified Plan certificate's attorney name; 72D prints the attorney block blank with no attorney, as the Clerk's forms do; 72E gives Schedule C and E negative amounts a keypad with a minus key; 72F splits the nine-form summary-page test; 72G keeps the method of service (PDF only, warned) apart from the ward's status (the workbook's Indicate if box, required), reads older workbooks' box by what it holds, and resets certificate answers on every new filing except recipients; 72H makes the certificate's attorney the filing's attorney, warns when the Inventory's two attorney names differ, keeps older workbooks' differing certificate details, and has Convert read a not-yet-opened source by the same one-time rules; 72I shows the Guardian Advocate hint at once; 72J rewords the missing-recipients issue; a follow-up makes the Annual-family and Simplified imports keep a guardian's signature state, stamp and served-the-copies tick for the same person, as 72H did for the Inventory; the sidebar Net Assets report is recorded as not reproduced; after the build (2026-10-03) the requester left the Annual's continuation pages as they are, had activity-log entries filed under their own filing, and had the release gate run. |
| [`MILESTONE-73-PROPOSAL.md`](<MILESTONE-73-PROPOSAL.md>) | Milestone 73 proposal (Draft; every decision settled 2026-10-04/05; authorizes no change except 73U, built in 5b8849b, 73V, built in 8b96aa3, 73C, built in 861b6a9, and 73D, built in c8e84fe). From the requester's end-to-end browser test of 2026-10-03, checked against the code, reviewed for accuracy, effectiveness and blast radius (2026-10-04) and by Codex (2026-10-05, three rounds), then consolidated and narrowed (73V a behaviour-preserving foundation plus the shifted-link fix; 73E part 1 workbook-independent; 73F part 1 with a compatibility contract): 22 items, each one final specification, the largest split into separately approved parts, foundations first (73V row rules, a model-change event, a page-draw reason, an amount codec, shared validators, an import transaction, a typed workbook contract). Items: signatures (Unsigned prints a blank line; guardians sign by hand or stamp), answers never given, Add Co-Guardian on the Plans, the vehicle box, Excel import as a transaction, one completeness rule, amounts, formats, due dates, live page parts, scroll and focus, dialogs, what Excel does not carry, PDF wrapping and court wording, cross-form consistency, small fixes, the user guide, the requester's change requests (sidebar height, "GF", preview zoom, the UCN), the Excel round trip, stored files cut by the import filter, and one description per repeating list. Appendices: the 2026-10-04 review, Codex's review, and every decision with its options. 2026-10-06 (73A): 73A's build record. 2026-10-06 (73F part 2): its build record. Milestone 72G: a same-period conversion carries the recipients and the ward's status and starts every other certificate answer, and the attorney's Part V signature date, blank; Inventory -> Simplified no longer carries the service date. 2026-10-03: the notes say only the certificate of service's recipients carry over (and the ward's status between accountings of one period), and that the attorney's details carry over. 2026-10-05 (73D): D-3 carries a B-2 vehicle's built description. 2026-10-05 (73J part 1): announces its changes through src/core/model-change.js. 2026-10-08 (73O part 2, 74F): recipients copy whole between forms (no fold, no dropped line). 2026-10-08 (73K part 1): says why it draws the page (draw-reason.js). 2026-10-09 (73O part 4, 74J): registry names and the workbook's Line 8; "filing type"; the notice says the source is unchanged; an Annual Plan from a plan says the residence carries. |

| [`src\core\persistence\case-file.js`](<src/core/persistence/case-file.js>) | The case file: building and writing .sav archives (buildCaseFileBlob(), writeCaseToHandle(), the Save Backup and single-filing exports), the open file's handle, the save-error banner, the auto-save interval's sweep and the last-saved readout; since Milestone 70's 70I also the save itself -- autoSave() (the case store's save hook), flushPendingSave(), saveData(), saveWardToState() -- and installSaveListeners(). Opening a file is case-reader.js; importing into the case, case-import.js. describeUnreadableParts() and protectPartiallyReadCaseFile() (master b2d97f5, carried): a partly read file is detached from auto-save and the filer told what was not read. A password-protected file's manifest lists filings without names (manifestWardEntry()), and newerCaseFileFormatMessage() refuses a newer format (master 5de3707, carried). |
| [`src\core\persistence\case-import.js`](<src/core/persistence/case-import.js>) | Milestone 70, 70I: opening a backup into the case, or importing a case file -- the Open Backup (.sav) button and a file dropped on the window: closes the filing being edited (through the filing lifecycle service), merges the file's filings and shared records, saves, and lands on the dashboard. Out of case-file.js, whose archive and save functions it uses. A backup with unreadable parts names them before the filer confirms, and never becomes the save file (master b2d97f5, carried). A file saved in a newer format is refused (master 5de3707, carried). 2026-10-08 (73K part 1): says why it draws the page (draw-reason.js). |
| [`src\core\persistence\case-reader.js`](<src/core/persistence/case-reader.js>) | Milestone 70, 70I: opening a case file (loadCaseFileFromZip()), the counterpart of case-file.js's buildCaseFileBlob(): filings, guardian, shared records, app preferences, the auto-save interval and last-save time, templates and the Activity Log, read out of a parsed .sav; a damaged filing or template entry is skipped. Moved from legacy-app.js; proven against the earlier versions' archives by tests/e2e/sav-corpus.characterization.spec.ts. It returns every part it could not read (master b2d97f5, carried). Since 70J the case is read into a case of its own, which replaces the open one once, whole (replaceCaseFile()): nothing sees a half-read case across the reads, and a read that fails part-way leaves the open case as it was. |
| [`src\core\persistence\crypto.js`](<src/core/persistence/crypto.js>) | Encryption at rest: PBKDF2 key derivation and AES-GCM (or the PLAIN: format) for every entry of a case file and the recovery snapshot, and -- since Milestone 70's 70I -- the session's key and security mode in this module's closure (getCryptoKey()/setCryptoKey()/clearCryptoKey(), getSecurityMode()/setSecurityMode()), never on window. Publishes nothing on window since 70K. |

Out of case-file.js (which re-exports part of it) so this tab's state for the other tabs reads it without importing the case file. |

| [`src\core\persistence\recovery-cache.js`](<src/core/persistence/recovery-cache.js>) | The recovery snapshot (pg-session-cache): the case written on every save while it has changes its file lacks -- filings, guardian and circuit, and since Milestone 70's 70I its shared records -- read back only by a lock in the same page (readOwnSessionRestoreCache(), which ignores a snapshot another tab or an earlier session left); and the last-position marker (pg-last-position). |

| [`src\core\runtime\features.js`](<src/core/runtime/features.js>) | Milestone 70, 70K: the feature services -- what core reaches of the filing features (mounting a feature's pages and sidebar, loading one, its validator once loaded, completion's inputs, a filing's headline total, a feature's own commands) -- provided once by startGuardianForms() from src/features-loader.js; features() throws if asked first. | [`src\core\security\app-lock.js`](<src/core/security/app-lock.js>) | Milestone 70, 70I: unlocking and locking the case -- choosing plain or password-protected storage, creating the password, the unlock prompt and its failure backoff, the password for a file being opened, locking on request and after 15 minutes idle (the case comes back from the file, or from this page's recovery snapshot when the file lacks changes), and the dialog's Enter key and activity listeners (installAppLockListeners()). A key is held only once verified and cleared on every lock and failed attempt. Moved from legacy-app.js. The re-read of the file after an unlock reports a part it cannot read and detaches the file (master b2d97f5, carried). A file saved in a newer format is refused (master 5de3707, carried). Since 70J the lock forgets the case through the store (replaceCaseFile(blankCaseFile())), and the recovery restore replaces it once, whole. Since 70K it draws the page after an unlock through the router (setRouteHash(), handleHash()). 2026-10-09 (73L): locking closes every pop-up first, each as a no. |

Since 2026-10-04 a strict base64 data URL (a stored signature stamp or supporting PDF) is left exactly as stored: the "on...=" strip used to cut the end off about 1 in 50 of them. 2026-10-07 (73T part 2): sanitizeImportedText(): sanitizeInput() keeping the quotation mark, for imported workbook text (73T-3). |

| [`src\core\state.js`](<src/core/state.js>) | The case-state seam (Milestone 70, 70E): every module reads and writes case state here, never through window -- the case file, the open filing (getD()/getActiveFiling()/setD(), which normalizes it with src/core/filing/normalize-filing.js since 70C), the open filing's type and record, app state and the template cache -- as the live objects the classic monolith still owns (window.X is written only here, the transition exception 70J removes), plus the store API new writes use: select(), transaction(reason, mutator) with its side effects once each (revision, save, subscribers) and subscribe(). The blank-filing factories it held moved to src/core/filing/models/ in 70C. Since 70G: setActiveFiling(), the one place a module changes which filing is open, and appStateObject(). Since 70I app state and the template cache are its own (no window copies), and the store's hooks gain requestSave() -- how every module asks for a save -- and commitPendingEdits(), both wired by main.js. Since 70J it is the case's owner, not a seam onto the monolith: it holds the one case object (getCaseFile(); replaceCaseFile(), the one way to replace it whole -- the lock, and opening a case file; blankCaseFile()), derives the open filing and its type from the case's activeWardId each time they are asked for (getD(), getActiveWard(), getActiveInventoryType() -- setD() and setActiveInventoryType() went), and puts a filing in view for GuardianForms.testing's validate queries (withFilingInView()). Nothing of the case is on window. Since 70K nothing on window reaches it at all (src/legacy-bridge.js went). 2026-10-06 (73A): opening a filing applies the signature policy (src/core/signature/signature-policy.js). |

| [`src\core\testing\testing-adapter.js`](<src/core/testing/testing-adapter.js>) | Milestone 70 (70T) window.GuardianForms.testing: the adapter browser specs use to reach app state -- installed only when the test runner set the pre-boot flag, read once and deleted (D3, T3); queries return copies, never live objects or key material; patchFiling() for setup and setField() for behavior (D9). A thin layer over today's globals whose internals follow the migration. Since Milestone 70's 70G its filing members call src/core/navigation/filing-lifecycle.js, and it imports the moved dialogs and describeConversion(). In 70H the Add Filing dialog, the tour and the sidebar refresh by import. In 70I its internals import what left the monolith (the save, the lock, the reader, the Activity Log, the key and save state), and countAutoSaves() swaps the case store's save hook. Since 70J it reads the case, the open filing and its type from the case store, and its validate queries put their filing in view (withFilingInView()) instead of pointing window.D at it. Since 70K its members run an explicit table of application functions (applicationImplementations(): imported, or a feature's own through the feature services) -- it looked each up on window -- validate queries load the type's feature first, simulate.validatorNotLoaded() holds for its own completion queries, saveOutput.pdfGuardian()/excelGuardian() work (the names they called were never defined), status.loadedFeatures() is new, and window.GuardianForms is src/core/runtime/browser-api.js's to install. Milestone 72A: excelWrites (start/stop the Excel write recorder) for the export guard. 2026-10-06 (73F part 1): validate.evaluate(), the open filing's evaluation by the shared export checks. 2026-10-06 (73F part 2): status.navChecks() and status.progress() read section-marks.js; completionDeps and simulate.validatorNotLoaded() retired. 2026-10-06 (73E part 1): importTransaction.plan/confirm/run -- the import transaction and its confirmation, before any importer uses them. 2026-10-06 (73F part 3): validate.requiredPaths(), what the asterisk drift test asks. 2026-10-07 (73T part 1): importTransaction.readWorkbook(): a workbook's bytes read by the open filing's workbook contract, as the adapter hands the transaction (a copy) -- for parts 2-4's browser tests. 2026-10-08 (73K part 1): says why it draws the page (draw-reason.js). |
